{"parentUuid":"b42a5ea0-4e5c-4f11-8998-7998dbc8e004","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JFR5w1bmFyFWgcbESiDwtJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL mobile bridge (Phase 7, WS-D D-v/vi) — the phone as an owner-authorized remote-control +\n2\tapproval surface over WireGuard. The engine stays on the owned desktop; the phone approves (with its\n3\town authorized device key), panic-halts, and relays KERNEL commands. Push carries only {seq,tier,kind}\n4\tover the tunnel — no subject, no secret, no cloud. Offense-free.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n6\t\n7\tassert_no_offense()\n8\t\n9\tfrom .daemon import BridgeDaemon, bind_ok  # noqa: E402\n10\tfrom .envelope import (  # noqa: E402\n11\t    ACTIONS,\n12\t    RECEIPT_SIGNAL,\n13\t    build_core,\n14\t    consume,\n15\t    device_nonce_highwater,\n16\t    envelope_message,\n17\t    record_receipt,\n18\t    sign_envelope,\n19\t    verify_envelope,\n20\t)\n21\tfrom .notifier import PushNotifier  # noqa: E402\n22\tfrom .server import BridgeServer, build_server, serve  # noqa: E402\n23\t\n24\t__all__ = [\"BridgeDaemon\", \"PushNotifier\", \"bind_ok\",\n25\t           \"ACTIONS\", \"RECEIPT_SIGNAL\", \"envelope_message\", \"build_core\", \"sign_envelope\",\n26\t           \"verify_envelope\", \"record_receipt\", \"device_nonce_highwater\", \"consume\",\n27\t           \"BridgeServer\", \"build_server\", \"serve\"]\n28\t"}]},"uuid":"aa9cb207-8305-4c7f-88d5-116f6ed1f487","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:02.188Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b42a5ea0-4e5c-4f11-8998-7998dbc8e004","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"aa9cb207-8305-4c7f-88d5-116f6ed1f487","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAUPNSa2UhB2Yb1EETAu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HTeEUkB8W41AhBriJWjMy7","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/gesture/session.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":14160,"cache_read_input_tokens":33082,"output_tokens":266,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":14160},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":266,"cache_read_input_tokens":33082,"cache_creation_input_tokens":14160,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":14160,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUPLgtzkjWYePEABiNX","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"a591c36e-9357-42b8-918b-30e3801c5820","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:02.353Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"a591c36e-9357-42b8-918b-30e3801c5820","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HTeEUkB8W41AhBriJWjMy7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SessionGate (Phase 8, WS-F, THE KEYSTONE) — the ONLY authority that injects input, and only inside\n2\tan owner-armed session. Two enforcement layers so a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended action:\n3\t\n4\t  Layer 1 — the SESSION: `arm()` REQUIRES the owner key (it cannot be armed without the owner\n5\t  identity) and appends a signed `gesture.session_armed` record — the tamper-evident AUDIT proof-of-\n6\t  authorization, indicator-lit. The INJECTION gate itself is the live in-memory session, held ONLY by\n7\t  the owner's own running daemon process; it is BOUNDED by a TTL and ended by disarm / hand-loss /\n8\t  expiry. Injection is REFUSED with no live session.\n9\t\n10\t  Layer 2 — per intent, the tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`hid.*` names): A1 (pointer\n11\t  move/click/scroll/drag) injects within the session; A2 (type/combo/app.launch) is QUEUED for a\n12\t  verified owner/device approval bound to `sha256(session_id|tool|args)` and NEVER auto-injected — so\n13\t  a gesture alone can never type a password or launch an app.\n14\t\n15\tOnly arm/disarm + DISCRETE actions (click/scroll/drag injected; type/launch queued) hit the spine —\n16\tper-frame pointer MOVES are telemetry (30 FPS records would DoS the append-only log).\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\timport time\n20\timport uuid\n21\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n22\tfrom typing import Optional\n23\t\n24\tfrom ..agents.base import Tier\n25\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n26\tfrom .components import InputBackend\n27\tfrom .types import GestureIntent\n28\t\n29\tSESSION_ARMED = \"gesture.session_armed\"\n30\tSESSION_DISARMED = \"gesture.session_disarmed\"\n31\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"gesture.action\"\n32\tARM_REQUEST = \"gesture.arm_request\"\n33\t\n34\tMAX_DEVICE_TTL = 300.0        # a DEVICE-armed session is deliberately SHORTER than a local owner arm (1800)\n35\tARM_FRESHNESS = 30.0          # a device arm request must be recent (anti-stale / bounds the replay window)\n36\t_ARM_CORE = (\"signal\", \"device_id\", \"nonce\", \"ts\", \"ttl_seconds\")\n37\t\n38\tINTENT_TOOL = {\n39\t    \"move\": \"hid.pointer.move\", \"click\": \"hid.pointer.click\",\n40\t    \"scroll_left\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\", \"scroll_right\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\",\n41\t    \"drag\": \"hid.pointer.drag\", \"type\": \"hid.type\", \"combo\": \"hid.combo\", \"launch\": \"hid.app.launch\",\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef arm_request_message(core: dict) -> bytes:\n46\t    \"\"\"The canonical bytes a device signs to request an arm — ONLY the signed core fields, so a caller\n47\t    cannot smuggle unsigned extras. Mirrors the approval/envelope canonical-json contract.\"\"\"\n48\t    from ..reuse import canonical_json\n49\t    m = canonical_json({k: core.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE})\n50\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode(\"utf-8\")\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef sign_arm_request(device_key, *, device_id: str, nonce, ts: float, ttl_seconds: float) -> dict:\n54\t    \"\"\"Phone-side: build a device-signed arm request. The device signs with its OWN key (never the owner\n55\t    trust-root); the owner authorized this device once via the mesh ledger. This is the credential the\n56\t    bridge records and `SessionGate.arm_by_device` verifies.\"\"\"\n57\t    from ..reuse import sign\n58\t    core = {\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"device_id\": device_id, \"nonce\": nonce, \"ts\": ts, \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds}\n59\t    return {**core, \"pubkey\": device_key.public_key_b64,\n60\t            \"sig\": sign(device_key.private_key_b64, arm_request_message(core))}\n61\t\n62\t\n63\t@dataclass\n64\tclass Session:\n65\t    session_id: str\n66\t    live: bool = True\n67\t    expires_at: float = 0.0            # wallclock deadline — a session is BOUNDED, never indefinite\n68\t\n69\t    def expired(self, now: float) -> bool:\n70\t        return self.expires_at > 0.0 and now >= self.expires_at\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tclass SessionGate:\n74\t    def __init__(self, store, backend: InputBackend, *, classifier=None, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n75\t        self.store = store\n76\t        self.backend = backend\n77\t        self._classifier = classifier\n78\t        self._owner_key = owner_key\n79\t        self._trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey\n80\t        self.session: Optional[Session] = None\n81\t\n82\t    def _trusted(self):\n83\t        if self._trusted_pubkey is None:\n84\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n85\t            self._trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()\n86\t        return self._trusted_pubkey\n87\t\n88\t    def _cls(self):\n89\t        if self._classifier is None:\n90\t            from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n91\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n92\t        return self._classifier\n93\t\n94\t    # --- Layer 1: the owner-armed session ---------------------------------------------------------\n95\t    def arm(self, *, owner_key=None, ttl_seconds: float = 1800.0) -> Session:\n96\t        from ..governor.authn import signed_payload\n97\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair\n98\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else (self._owner_key or owner_keypair())\n99\t        if ok is None:\n100\t            raise RuntimeError(\"cannot arm a gesture session without the owner key (BLOCK-3)\")\n101\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n102\t        core = {\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid}\n103\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n104\t                          payload={**signed_payload(core, ok), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n105\t                                   \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds,\n106\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED (indicator lit — opt-in)\"})\n107\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=time.time() + ttl_seconds)\n108\t        return self.session\n109\t\n110\t    def disarm(self) -> None:\n111\t        if self.session is not None:\n112\t            self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n113\t                              payload={\"signal\": SESSION_DISARMED, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n114\t                                       \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"summary\": \"gesture session DISARMED\"})\n115\t            self.session.live = False\n116\t        self.session = None\n117\t\n118\t    # --- Layer 1b: DEVICE-signed remote arm (a deliberate, reviewed trust-widening) ----------------\n119\t    def arm_by_device(self, request: dict, *, now: Optional[float] = None) -> Optional[Session]:\n120\t        \"\"\"Arm on a VERIFIED, owner-authorized DEVICE signature (SIGIL §remote-arm). Every downstream\n121\t        bound is UNCHANGED — the A1-inject/A2-queue tier gate, the TTL, hand-loss/expiry disarm, and an\n122\t        owner disarm/kill that always wins. Fail-closed at each step; a refusal is recorded, never armed.\"\"\"\n123\t        from ..governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n124\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n125\t        from ..reuse import verify_one\n126\t        now = time.time() if now is None else now\n127\t        # (1) kill-switch FIRST — an owner halt (incl. a phone panic) beats any arm.\n128\t        if KillSwitch(self.store).is_engaged():\n129\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"kill-switch engaged\")\n130\t        # (2) owner-minted device key + valid signature over the signed core (RP-APPROVAL-2: the\n131\t        #     authorized set is owner-signed only, so a merely-presented key is never trusted).\n132\t        if request.get(\"signal\") != ARM_REQUEST:\n133\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"not an arm request\")\n134\t        pub = request.get(\"pubkey\")\n135\t        authorized = authorized_devices(self.store, self._trusted())\n136\t        core = {k: request.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE}\n137\t        try:                                            # verify_one RAISES on a malformed-length sig — treat as refusal\n138\t            sig_ok = bool(pub) and pub in authorized and verify_one(pub, arm_request_message(core), request.get(\"sig\", \"\"))\n139\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — any crypto/decoding error is a fail-closed refusal, never a crash\n140\t            sig_ok = False\n141\t        if not sig_ok:\n142\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"unauthorized or invalid device signature\")\n143\t        # (3) freshness — bounds the replay window and rejects a stale captured request.\n144\t        try:\n145\t            ts = float(request.get(\"ts\"))\n146\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n147\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"missing/invalid timestamp\")\n148\t        if abs(now - ts) > ARM_FRESHNESS:\n149\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"stale arm request\")\n150\t        # (4) replay — this (device, nonce) must not have armed before (the append-only spine is witness).\n151\t        nonce = request.get(\"nonce\")\n152\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n153\t            p = r.payload\n154\t            if (p.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and p.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\"\n155\t                    and p.get(\"device_pubkey\") == pub and p.get(\"nonce\") == nonce):\n156\t                return self._refuse_arm(request, \"replayed arm nonce\")\n157\t        # (5) single live session — a device arm NEVER displaces an existing/owner session.\n158\t        if self.session is not None and self.session.live and not self.session.expired(now):\n159\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"a gesture session is already live\")\n160\t        # (6) TTL clamp — shorter than a local owner arm; a deliberate trust-narrowing inside the widening.\n161\t        try:\n162\t            ttl = min(float(request.get(\"ttl_seconds\", MAX_DEVICE_TTL)), MAX_DEVICE_TTL)\n163\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n164\t            ttl = MAX_DEVICE_TTL\n165\t        if ttl <= 0:\n166\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"non-positive ttl\")\n167\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n168\t        # the record's AUTHORIZATION is the verified device signature (self-verifying vs the owner-signed\n169\t        # device ledger) — not the owner key; actor=DEVICE mirrors submit_device_approval.\n170\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n171\t                          payload={\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid, \"armed_by\": \"device\",\n172\t                                   \"device_id\": core[\"device_id\"], \"device_pubkey\": pub, \"nonce\": nonce,\n173\t                                   \"ts\": ts, \"sig\": request.get(\"sig\"), \"pubkey\": pub,\n174\t                                   \"authorization\": \"device-signed\", \"ttl_seconds\": ttl,\n175\t                                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n176\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED by authorized device (remote)\"})\n177\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=now + ttl)\n178\t        return self.session\n179\t\n180\t    def _refuse_arm(self, request: dict, reason: str) -> None:\n181\t        self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n182\t                          payload={\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"tier\": \"A0\",\n183\t                                   \"device_id\": request.get(\"device_id\"), \"reason\": reason,\n184\t                                   \"summary\": f\"device gesture arm refused: {reason}\"})\n185\t        return None\n186\t\n187\t    # --- Layer 2: per-intent tier gate ------------------------------------------------------------\n188\t    def handle(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> dict:\n189\t        \"\"\"Authorize + act on ONE intent. Returns a verdict dict. Injection ONLY inside a live session\n190\t        and ONLY for A1 tools; A2+ queues for approval and injects NOTHING; a danger name → refused.\"\"\"\n191\t        if intent.kind == \"hand_lost\":\n192\t            self.disarm()\n193\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"hand lost — session disarmed\"}\n194\t        # An owner halt (incl. a phone panic_engage) instantly neuters injection mid-session — critical\n195\t        # for a device-armed session the owner may not be watching. Consulted per intent (fail-closed).\n196\t        from ..governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n197\t        if KillSwitch(self.store).is_engaged():\n198\t            self.disarm()\n199\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"kill-switch engaged — session disarmed\"}\n200\t        if self.session is None or not self.session.live:\n201\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"no armed session — injection refused\"}\n202\t        if self.session.expired(time.time()):             # BLOCK-2: a session is bounded, never indefinite\n203\t            self.disarm()\n204\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"session expired — disarmed\"}\n205\t        tool = INTENT_TOOL.get(intent.kind)\n206\t        if tool is None:\n207\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": f\"unknown intent {intent.kind}\"}\n208\t        tier = self._cls().classify(tool)                 # DERIVED, fail-closed A3 on any error\n209\t        args = f\"{intent.dx:.4f},{intent.dy:.4f},{intent.arg}\"\n210\t        if tier <= Tier.A1:\n211\t            self._inject(intent)\n212\t            # BLOCK-1: log the DISCRETE A1 injections that actually take effect (click/scroll/drag);\n213\t            # per-frame `move` is telemetry (logging it at 30 FPS would DoS the append-only spine).\n214\t            if intent.kind != \"move\":\n215\t                self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n216\t                                  payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n217\t                                           \"tool\": tool, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n218\t                                           \"summary\": f\"gesture {tool} injected\"})\n219\t            return {\"injected\": True, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool}\n220\t        # A2/A3 → QUEUE an approval bound to this exact action; inject NOTHING\n221\t        token = sha256_hex(f\"{self.session.session_id}|{tool}|{args}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n222\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"PERCEPTION\",\n223\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"queued\",\n224\t                                         \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n225\t                                         \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token, \"args\": args,\n226\t                                         \"subject\": f\"gesture action {tool} (awaiting approval)\"})\n227\t        return {\"injected\": False, \"queued\": seq, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token}\n228\t\n229\t    def _inject(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> None:\n230\t        b = self.backend\n231\t        if intent.kind == \"move\" or intent.kind == \"drag\":\n232\t            b.move(intent.dx, intent.dy)\n233\t        elif intent.kind == \"click\":\n234\t            b.click()\n235\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_left\":\n236\t            b.scroll(-1, 0)\n237\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_right\":\n238\t            b.scroll(1, 0)\n239\t"}]},"uuid":"0d49f5f8-49a4-43c6-ae6c-5033bfa8bc8c","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:02.381Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a591c36e-9357-42b8-918b-30e3801c5820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

The PHONE runs its OWN on-device hand-\n2\tlandmark detection and streams tiny `List[Hand]` batches (landmark DATA, never owner pixels) to the PC\n3\tover the owner's own tunnel; the PC feeds them through the EXISTING gesture pipeline. This slice streams\n4\tinto a LOCAL owner-armed session ONLY — the owner arms at the PC (`run_gesture(auto_arm=False, gate=<armed>)`)\n5\tand the phone supplies landmarks. The device-signed REMOTE arm is a SEPARATE, later-authorized slice and\n6\tis deliberately NOT built here.\n7\t\n8\tDesign (mirrors the on-box components; NO dependency on `sigil/bridge/` — an inbound batch iterable is\n9\tINJECTED, so there is no socket/import coupling here):\n10\t\n11\t  • `decode_hand_batch(obj) -> List[Hand]` — parse+VALIDATE the compact wire form; on ANY malformation\n12\t    return an HONEST `[]` (never a fabricated hand), exactly as `OnnxHandLandmarker.detect` returns `[]`\n13\t    on failure.\n14\t  • `RemoteLandmarkSource` — the frame source: wraps the injected inbound feed, drops foreign-session /\n15\t    replayed / reordered / stale batches, and yields ONE token (the decoded `List[Hand]`) per ACCEPTED\n16\t    batch. It tracks only a single last-accepted `seq` (a bounded int — no unbounded buffer), and because\n17\t    `seq` is strictly monotonic an out-of-order (older) batch arriving after a newer one is dropped\n18\t    (drop-to-latest).\n19\t  • `RemoteLandmarker` — a `LandmarkModel` with `egresses = False` (HONEST: it uploads NO owner pixels;\n20\t    the phone already did the inference and sends inbound landmark DATA). `detect(token)` returns the\n21\t    token's hands, ignoring any image.\n22\t\"\"\"\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\timport math\n26\timport time\n27\tfrom typing import Iterable, Iterator, List, Optional\n28\t\n29\tfrom .types import Hand\n30\t\n31\tWIRE_TYPE = \"hand_batch\"\n32\tMAX_HANDS = 2\n33\tN_LANDMARKS = 21\n34\t_COORD_CAP = 8.0          # sane bound: reject NaN/inf/garbage coordinates (normalized hands live near [0,1])\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef _is_number(v) -> bool:\n38\t    # a real, finite number — bools are ints in Python, so exclude them explicitly\n39\t    return isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and math.isfinite(float(v))\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef _valid_coord(v) -> bool:\n43\t    return _is_number(v) and abs(float(v)) <= _COORD_CAP\n44\t\n45\t\n46\tdef decode_hand_batch(obj) -> List[Hand]:\n47\t    \"\"\"Decode a compact wire batch to `List[Hand]`, VALIDATING strictly. Wire form:\n48\t\n49\t        {\"t\":\"hand_batch\",\"session_id\":str,\"seq\":int,\"ts\":float,\n50\t         \"hands\":[ {\"l\":[[x,y,z] × 21], \"h\":\"R\"|\"L\", \"s\":score}, ... ≤ 2 ]}\n51\t\n52\t    Rules (any violation ⇒ return `[]`, honest-empty — never fabricate):\n53\t      • obj is a dict tagged `\"t\":\"hand_batch\"`;\n54\t      • `hands` is a list of AT MOST 2 hands;\n55\t      • each hand has EXACTLY 21 landmarks, each a 3-tuple of finite, sane (|v|≤8) floats;\n56\t      • handedness `h` ∈ {\"R\",\"L\"} (→ \"Right\"/\"Left\"), defaulting \"R\" only when the key is absent;\n57\t      • score `s` a finite float in [0,1], defaulting 1.0 only when the key is absent.\n58\t    \"\"\"\n59\t    try:\n60\t        if not isinstance(obj, dict) or obj.get(\"t\") != WIRE_TYPE:\n61\t            return []\n62\t        raw_hands = obj.get(\"hands\")\n63\t        if not isinstance(raw_hands, list) or len(raw_hands) > MAX_HANDS:\n64\t            return []\n65\t        out: List[Hand] = []\n66\t        for rh in raw_hands:\n67\t            if not isinstance(rh, dict):\n68\t                return []\n69\t            lms = rh.get(\"l\")\n70\t            if not isinstance(lms, (list, tuple)) or len(lms) != N_LANDMARKS:\n71\t                return []\n72\t            pts: List[tuple] = []\n73\t            for p in lms:\n74\t                if not isinstance(p, (list, tuple)) or len(p) != 3:\n75\t                    return []\n76\t                if not (_valid_coord(p[0]) and _valid_coord(p[1]) and _valid_coord(p[2])):\n77\t                    return []\n78\t                pts.append((float(p[0]), float(p[1]), float(p[2])))\n79\t            h = rh.get(\"h\", \"R\")\n80\t            if h not in (\"R\", \"L\"):\n81\t                return []\n82\t            handed = \"Right\" if h == \"R\" else \"Left\"\n83\t            s = rh.get(\"s\", 1.0)\n84\t            if not _is_number(s) or not (0.0 <= float(s) <= 1.0):\n85\t                return []\n86\t            out.append(Hand(landmarks=tuple(pts), handedness=handed, score=float(s)))\n87\t        return out\n88\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — ANY malformation ⇒ honest empty (mirror OnnxHandLandmarker.detect)\n89\t        return []\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tclass ScriptedRemoteSource:\n93\t    \"\"\"Deterministic double — an injected inbound feed of raw wire dicts (no socket, no `bridge`).\n94\t    Mirrors `perception.camera_stream.ScriptedFrameSource`: replays a fixed list of batch objects.\"\"\"\n95\t\n96\t    def __init__(self, batches: Iterable):\n97\t        self._batches = list(batches)\n98\t\n99\t    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator:\n100\t        return iter(self._batches)\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tclass RemoteLandmarkSource:\n104\t    \"\"\"Frame source over an INJECTED inbound batch feed, bound to the LIVE local session.\n105\t\n106\t    `frames()` yields ONE token (the decoded `List[Hand]`) per ACCEPTED batch. A batch is REJECTED\n107\t    (dropped, never fabricated) when its `session_id` ≠ the live session, its `seq` ≤ the last accepted\n108\t    `seq` (duplicate / reorder / replay), or — if a freshness window is set — its `ts` is outside it.\n109\t    The paired `RemoteLandmarker` returns the yielded token, so the token IS the landmark payload.\"\"\"\n110\t\n111\t    def __init__(self, inbound: Iterable, session_id: str, *,\n112\t                 freshness_seconds: Optional[float] = None, now=time.time):\n113\t        self._inbound = inbound\n114\t        self.session_id = session_id\n115\t        self._freshness = freshness_seconds\n116\t        self._now = now\n117\t        self._last_seq: Optional[int] = None   # bounded: a single int, never an unbounded accepted-set\n118\t\n119\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n120\t        return True\n121\t\n122\t    def _accept(self, obj) -> Optional[List[Hand]]:\n123\t        \"\"\"Return the decoded hands for an ACCEPTED batch, or None if the batch is rejected.\"\"\"\n124\t        if not isinstance(obj, dict) or obj.get(\"t\") != WIRE_TYPE:\n125\t            return None\n126\t        if obj.get(\"session_id\") != self.session_id:            # foreign session → drop\n127\t            return None\n128\t        seq = obj.get(\"seq\")\n129\t        if not isinstance(seq, int) or isinstance(seq, bool):   # seq must be a real int\n130\t            return None\n131\t        if self._last_seq is not None and seq <= self._last_seq:   # replay / reorder / duplicate → drop\n132\t            return None\n133\t        if self._freshness is not None:                          # optional freshness window\n134\t            ts = obj.get(\"ts\")\n135\t            if not _is_number(ts) or abs(self._now() - float(ts)) > self._freshness:\n136\t                return None\n137\t        hands = decode_hand_batch(obj)   # may be [] (honest) — a valid envelope with malformed hands\n138\t        self._last_seq = seq             # advance ONLY once the batch is accepted at the envelope level\n139\t        return hands\n140\t\n141\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator[List[Hand]]:\n142\t        for obj in self._inbound:\n143\t            hands = self._accept(obj)\n144\t            if hands is None:            # rejected — note: [] is ACCEPTED (honest no-hand), None is not\n145\t                continue\n146\t            yield hands\n147\t\n148\t\n149\tclass RemoteLandmarker:\n150\t    \"\"\"A `LandmarkModel` fed by the phone's OWN on-device inference. `egresses = False` is HONEST —\n151\t    it uploads NO owner pixels; the inbound stream is landmark DATA over the owner's own tunnel. Because\n152\t    `egresses is False`, the Wave-1 fail-closed egress gate in `run_gesture` PASSES it (reconciliation:\n153\t    that gate refuses a model that would EGRESS owner imagery; this one egresses nothing).\"\"\"\n154\t\n155\t    egresses = False\n156\t    source_kind = \"remote_device_stream\"\n157\t\n158\t    def detect(self, token) -> List[Hand]:\n159\t        # `token` is the decoded List[Hand] yielded by RemoteLandmarkSource.frames(); return it as-is,\n160\t        # ignoring any image (the phone already produced these landmarks). Honest-empty otherwise.\n161\t        if isinstance(token, list):\n162\t            return token\n163\t        return []\n164\t"}]},"uuid":"3bb3f1c0-3f25-4bd8-ab82-322c06d65381","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:09.124Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0e3c5eb2-d3b6-4911-875f-e5df663c2489","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8a397d35-ff89-4870-86e3-4ae80214c79a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Y1gF5uVMM8UPbLGsuR2uUt","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The live gesture-control daemon (Phase 8, WS-F F7) — camera stream → landmarks → classify → FSM →\n2\tSessionGate, mirroring `voice/run.py run_mic`. Arms the session on start (indicator lit), disarms in a\n3\t`finally` (indicator cleared even on crash). Graceful fallbacks; components are injectable so the\n4\twhole loop runs deterministically on scripted doubles with no camera/model/OS input.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom .pipeline import GesturePipeline\n10\tfrom .session import SessionGate\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef run_gesture(*, store=None, owner_key=None, source=None, landmarker=None, classifier=None,\n14\t                backend=None, pipeline=None, gate=None, max_frames: Optional[int] = None,\n15\t                auto_arm: bool = True) -> int:\n16\t    \"\"\"Run the loop; returns the number of frames processed. Injectable for tests.\n17\t\n18\t    `auto_arm=True` (default) is the camera path: owner-arm the session locally on start. `auto_arm=False`\n19\t    is for a caller that passes an ALREADY-ARMED `gate` — e.g. a LOCAL owner-armed session consuming a\n20\t    `remote.RemoteLandmarkSource` (owner arms at the PC; the phone streams landmarks in). This adds NO\n21\t    device-arm path; the owner still arms locally, and every other guarantee (egress gate, Layer-1\n22\t    session, Layer-2 tier gate, disarm-in-finally) is unchanged.\"\"\"\n23\t    if store is None:\n24\t        from ..spine.store import SpineStore\n25\t        store = SpineStore()\n26\t    if source is None:\n27\t        from ..perception.camera_stream import CameraStreamSource\n28\t        source = CameraStreamSource()\n29\t    if landmarker is None:\n30\t        from .landmark import OnnxHandLandmarker\n31\t        landmarker = OnnxHandLandmarker()\n32\t    # STRICT fail-closed egress gate (Phase 9 W2-H): this loop injects HID off a live camera stream,\n33\t    # so it refuses UNLESS the landmarker is EXPLICITLY on-box (`egresses is False`) — stricter than\n34\t    # perceive's default-safe `getattr(..., \"egresses\", False)` form, because an unset/unknown flag\n35\t    # here would mean streaming owner PIXELS off-box while driving the pointer. This gate refuses a\n36\t    # model that would EGRESS owner imagery; a future `RemoteLandmarker` with `egresses=False` (an\n37\t    # authorized INBOUND landmark-DATA stream over the owner's own tunnel) is a DISTINCT,\n38\t    # separately-authorized thing — it egresses nothing and passes this gate.\n39\t    if getattr(landmarker, \"egresses\", True) is not False:\n40\t        store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n41\t                     payload={\"signal\": \"gesture.refused\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"tier\": \"A0\",\n42\t                              \"reason\": \"landmark model would egress owner imagery — the gesture \"\n43\t                                        \"loop is on-box only\"})\n44\t        return 0  # inject NOTHING; never armed\n45\t    if classifier is None:\n46\t        from .features import RuleClassifier\n47\t        classifier = RuleClassifier()\n48\t    if backend is None:\n49\t        from ..platform import input_backend\n50\t        backend = input_backend()\n51\t    pipe = pipeline or GesturePipeline()\n52\t    g = gate or SessionGate(store, backend, owner_key=owner_key)\n53\t    if auto_arm:\n54\t        g.arm(owner_key=owner_key)   # camera path: owner-arm locally. When False, the caller's gate is\n55\t        # already armed (a LOCAL owner-armed session) and we consume it without a second arm.\n56\t    n = 0\n57\t    try:\n58\t        for frame in source.frames():\n59\t            reading = classifier.classify(landmarker.detect(frame))\n60\t            intent = pipe.on_frame(reading)\n61\t            if intent is not None:\n62\t                g.handle(intent)\n63\t            n += 1\n64\t            if max_frames is not None and n >= max_frames:\n65\t                break\n66\t    finally:\n67\t        g.disarm()\n68\t    return n\n69\t"}]},"uuid":"2dd69b38-3030-4bbb-b483-b1d31071ef9f","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:09.463Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8a397d35-ff89-4870-86e3-4ae80214c79a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3084b223-7bf3-460e-922f-bd480ede51bd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XaQDfLvUoncfHkL64FkGm2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom typing import Optional\n12\t\n13\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n14\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n15\t\n16\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n17\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass KillSwitch:\n21\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n22\t        self.store = store\n23\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n24\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n25\t\n26\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n27\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n28\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n29\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n30\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n31\t\n32\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n33\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n34\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n35\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n39\t        engaged = False\n40\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n41\t            p = r.payload\n42\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n43\t                continue\n44\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n45\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n46\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n47\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n48\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n49\t        return engaged\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"a3ab55f6-8d7e-476f-9e1c-60f67f43bf72","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:09.713Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3084b223-7bf3-460e-922f-bd480ede51bd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"aec15ab0-cbbd-4e68-8f16-613548e88ae8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015As4qdMjbMaGfsPHeR224E","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n17\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n18\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n22\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n23\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n24\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n25\t        end = f.tell()\n26\t        if end == 0:\n27\t            return None\n28\t        buf = b\"\"\n29\t        pos = end\n30\t        while pos > 0:\n31\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n32\t            pos -= step\n33\t            f.seek(pos)\n34\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n35\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n36\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n37\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n38\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n39\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass SpineStore:\n43\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n44\t        self.path = Path(path)\n45\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n47\t\n48\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\t    def append(\n50\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n51\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n52\t        ts: str | None = None,\n53\t    ) -> int:\n54\t        content = {\n55\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n56\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n57\t        }\n58\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n59\t        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n60\t        record = {\n61\t            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n62\t            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n63\t        }\n64\t        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n65\t            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n66\t        self._last = entry\n67\t        return entry.seq\n68\t\n69\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n71\t        if not self.path.exists():\n72\t            return\n73\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n74\t            for line in f:\n75\t                line = line.strip()\n76\t                if not line:\n77\t                    continue\n78\t                d = json.loads(line)\n79\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n80\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n81\t\n82\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n83\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n84\t            if r.seq == seq:\n85\t                return r\n86\t            if r.seq > seq:\n87\t                break\n88\t        return None\n89\t\n90\t    @property\n91\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n92\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n93\t\n94\t    def count(self) -> int:\n95\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n96\t\n97\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n98\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n99\t        return [\n100\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n101\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n102\t        ]\n103\t\n104\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n105\t        \"\"\"Two-layer UNKEYED integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n106\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link cleanly\n107\t        (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). This proves internal CONSISTENCY, not authenticity:\n108\t        a naive payload edit fails (1), and a mid-chain digest edit cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash\n109\t        break caught by (2) — BUT a writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash for the tip (no\n110\t        successor to cascade into) or forward-cascades a fork produces a self-consistent chain that\n111\t        passes here. Resistance to a recompute-capable writer is the owner-SIGNED head's job\n112\t        (`checkpoint.verify_checkpoint`, Ed25519 + monotonic last_seq). Use this for corruption/\n113\t        naive-tamper detection; use the signed head for tamper-EVIDENCE.\"\"\"\n114\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n115\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n116\t            content = {\n117\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n118\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n119\t            }\n120\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n121\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n122\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n123\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n124\t\n125\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n126\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n127\t        if not line:\n128\t            return None\n129\t        d = json.loads(line)\n130\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n131\t"}]},"uuid":"f41adcd6-4539-4fc5-b805-dee5cd88ff6c","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:15.747Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"aec15ab0-cbbd-4e68-8f16-613548e88ae8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f41adcd6-4539-4fc5-b805-dee5cd88ff6c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAUQG2NzQACxPPkAkaJy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01M8KSM11ox9bsSmVne7VvFn","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/approvals.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6365,"cache_read_input_tokens":56627,"output_tokens":266,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6365},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":266,"cache_read_input_tokens":56627,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6365,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6365,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUQESdmzayNyhEykbSM","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"7d523307-aff4-47cf-972d-c6fd5454e528","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:16.391Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"7d523307-aff4-47cf-972d-c6fd5454e528","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M8KSM11ox9bsSmVne7VvFn","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"601442df-c5f9-4580-9da8-8bc125508e24","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:31:16.422Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7d523307-aff4-47cf-972d-c6fd5454e528","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"a4f7d1e6-b4ec-4ba2-996d-c62f825e04a9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NACipjQBAQAMwp26rB7STC","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"KernelClassifier (Phase 7, P0.1) — the Python↔Rust WARDEN bridge. The authoritative danger\n2\tclassifier is Rust `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3),\n3\tcomposed with the raise-only registry pins in `Warden::decide`. Python's `Governor` otherwise\n4\tTRUSTS a Proposal's declared tier; this bridge lets the mesh DERIVE a tool's tier from that\n5\tfail-closed oracle instead of self-declaring it — so an Operator step or a Vision egress hop is\n6\ttiered by the same code that enforces at the kernel.\n7\t\n8\tConsulting the kernel is a pure A0 observation (`sigil-kernel classify` writes NO action-log\n9\trecord). Any failure — missing binary, timeout, non-zero exit, unparseable output, or an unknown\n10\ttier string — resolves to **A3** (fail-closed to the most-gated tier), mirroring the Rust\n11\tclassifier's own posture: never let a bridge error silently downgrade a dangerous tool.\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport json\n15\timport subprocess\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..voice.dispatch import _default_bin  # reuse the exact kernel-binary resolution\n18\tfrom .base import Tier\n19\t\n20\t_TIER = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass KernelClassifier:\n24\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n25\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n26\t        self.timeout = timeout\n27\t\n28\t    def classify(self, tool: str) -> Tier:\n29\t        \"\"\"Return the WARDEN tier for `tool`. Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\n30\t        if not tool or not tool.strip():\n31\t            return Tier.A3\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n36\t            return Tier.A3\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            return Tier.A3\n39\t        try:\n40\t            data = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])\n41\t            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\n42\t        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n43\t            return Tier.A3\n44\t"}]},"uuid":"4a75d5cf-dfa1-4b0b-93ff-3116f4ea70f4","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:32:04.087Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a4f7d1e6-b4ec-4ba2-996d-c62f825e04a9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f9f10456-b131-457a-9d8f-36705bf52e91","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WKGaPf5P1dk6eH97QyjJYn","type":"tool_result","content":"diff --git a/sigil/cli.py b/sigil/cli.py\nindex 2aa50a1..df036db 100644\n--- a/sigil/cli.py\n+++ b/sigil/cli.py\n@@ -9,6 +9,8 @@ from .ingest import cursor as cur\n from .ingest.corpus import real_projects, session_files\n from .ingest.docs import ingest_docs\n from .ingest.transcript import ingest_transcript\n+from .mesh import authorize_device, authorized_devices, revoke_device\n+from .reuse import sha256_hex\n from .spine.checkpoint import checkpoint, verify_checkpoint\n from .spine.store import SpineStore\n from .vectors.index import VectorIndex\n@@ -361,6 +363,69 @@ def cmd_host(a) -> None:\n     print(f\"    screen={d.has_screen} camera={d.has_camera} gpu_vlm={d.has_gpu_vlm} always_on={d.always_on}\")\n \n \n+def _device_fingerprint(pubkey: str) -> str:\n+    \"\"\"A short, human-verifiable fingerprint of a device pubkey — first 8 bytes of its SHA-256 as\n+    `xxxx-xxxx-xxxx-xxxx`. The PC and the future phone client MUST compute this identically so the\n+    operator can eyeball-match the code the phone shows before authorizing (defeats a key swap).\"\"\"\n+    short = sha256_hex(pubkey.encode())[:16]\n+    return \"-\".join(short[i:i + 4] for i in range(0, 16, 4))\n+\n+\n+def _mesh_authorize(store, device_id, pubkey, owner_key, *, assume_yes=False, confirm=input):\n+    \"\"\"Authorize a phone device key (owner-signed). Prints the fingerprint and, unless `assume_yes`,\n+    makes the operator confirm it matches what the phone displays before writing the ledger record.\n+    Returns the record seq, or None if the operator did not confirm.\"\"\"\n+    fp = _device_fingerprint(pubkey)\n+    print(f\"  device {device_id!r} fingerprint: {fp}\")\n+    if not assume_yes:\n+        ans = confirm(f\"  confirm this fingerprint matches what phone {device_id!r} shows? [yes/no] \")\n+        if (ans or \"\").strip().lower() != \"yes\":\n+            print(\"  aborted — fingerprint not confirmed; device NOT authorized\")\n+            return None\n+    seq = authorize_device(store, device_id, pubkey, owner_key)\n+    print(f\"  authorized device {device_id!r} (owner-signed, seq {seq}); fingerprint {fp}\")\n+    return seq\n+\n+\n+def _mesh_revoke(store, device_id, pubkey, owner_key):\n+    \"\"\"Revoke a phone device key (owner-signed). Returns the record seq.\"\"\"\n+    seq = revoke_device(store, device_id, pubkey, owner_key)\n+    print(f\"  revoked device {device_id!r} (owner-signed, seq {seq}); fingerprint {_device_fingerprint(pubkey)}\")\n+    return seq\n+\n+\n+def _mesh_list(store, owner_pubkey):\n+    \"\"\"Print the currently-authorized device keys (owner-signed authorize minus later revoke).\n+    Returns the set for callers/tests.\"\"\"\n+    devices = authorized_devices(store, owner_pubkey)\n+    if not devices:\n+        print(\"  no authorized devices\")\n+    else:\n+        print(f\"  {len(devices)} authorized device key(s):\")\n+        for pub in sorted(devices):\n+            print(f\"    {pub}  [{_device_fingerprint(pub)}]\")\n+    return devices\n+\n+\n+def cmd_mesh(a) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Phase 9 W1-E — the PC-side mesh pairing back-end: authorize / revoke / list phone device keys.\n+    Authorizations are owner-signed via the PERSISTED owner identity (never a supplied key); a thin\n+    wrapper over the tested mesh ledger. `list-devices` needs no signing (a fail-closed read).\"\"\"\n+    from .governor.identity import ensure_owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n+    store = SpineStore()\n+    if a.action == \"list-devices\":\n+        _mesh_list(store, owner_pubkey())\n+        return\n+    if not a.device_id or not a.pubkey:\n+        print(f\"  usage: sigil mesh {a.action} <device-id> <pubkey>\", file=sys.stderr)\n+        sys.exit(2)\n+    owner = ensure_owner_keypair()   # the persisted owner key IS the trusted signer (doctrine)\n+    if a.action == \"authorize\":\n+        _mesh_authorize(store, a.device_id, a.pubkey, owner, assume_yes=a.yes)\n+    elif a.action == \"revoke\":\n+        _mesh_revoke(store, a.device_id, a.pubkey, owner)\n+\n+\n def cmd_serve(a) -> None:\n     \"\"\"Start the loopback glass-cockpit UI. Binds a `bind_ok` address\n+    (loopback or a PRIVATE WireGuard IP) — FAIL-CLOSED (exit 2) on a public/unspecified addr, minting\n+    no cert — and, unless --no-tls, wraps it in an owner-pinned self-signed TLS cert so the phone gets\n+    the secure context a PWA needs to install + register a service worker (pin the printed fingerprint\n+    once). Minimum browsers with Ed25519 in\n11\t * crypto.subtle:  Chrome/Edge 137+ · Safari 17+ · Firefox 129+ · Node 22 (for the parity test).\n12\t * If crypto.subtle lacks Ed25519 the app says so honestly and refuses to operate.\n13\t *\n14\t * ENCODING PARITY with sigil (sigil/reuse/crypto.py):\n15\t *   - device PUBLIC key (raw 32 bytes, exportKey(\"raw\")) and SIGNATURES (raw 64 bytes) are STANDARD\n16\t *     base64 WITH padding (b64std) — because verify_one() -> _b64decode_exact() does\n17\t *     base64.b64decode(value, validate=True), which only accepts the standard alphabet (+ / =).\n18\t *   - the WHOLE envelope JSON that rides in X-SIGIL-Envelope / ?env= is base64URL (b64url), because\n19\t *     the server decodes it with base64.urlsafe_b64decode (padding re-added server-side).\n20\t *   - canonical signing bytes come from SigilCanonical.canonicalJson (see canonical.js — the parity\n21\t *     contract). ts is floored to INTEGER seconds and nonces/seqs are integers so the JS and Python\n22\t *     canonical bytes are byte-identical (floats are never signed).\n23\t *   - the human fingerprint = grouped SHA-256(pubkey-base64-STRING) first 8 bytes as xxxx-xxxx-xxxx-\n24\t *     xxxx, matching sigil/cli.py :: _device_fingerprint (which hashes pubkey.encode(), i.e. the\n25\t *     base64 STRING, NOT the raw key bytes).\n26\t *\n27\t * CSP: default-src 'self'. No inline scripts, no inline event handlers, no external origins. All\n28\t * behaviour is wired by ONE delegated click listener + a few input handlers.\n29\t */\n30\t\n31\t/* ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- tiny DOM helpers */\n32\tvar $ = function (s) { return document.querySelector(s); };\n33\tvar esc = function (s) {\n34\t  return String(s == null ? \"\" : s).replace(/[&<>]/g, function (c) {\n35\t    return { \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\" }[c];\n36\t  });\n37\t};\n38\tvar enc = new TextEncoder();\n39\tfunction banner(kind, html) {\n40\t  var b = $(\"#banner\");\n41\t  b.className = \"banner \" + kind;\n42\t  b.innerHTML = html;\n43\t}\n44\tfunction clearBanner() { $(\"#banner\").className = \"banner info hidden\"; }\n45\t\n46\t/* ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- base64 */\n47\tfunction bytesToB64std(bytes) {                       // standard base64, WITH padding (pubkey / sig)\n48\t  var s = \"\";\n49\t  for (var i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) { s += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]); }\n50\t  return btoa(s);\n51\t}\n52\tfunction strToB64url(str) {                           // base64URL, no padding (the envelope wrapper)\n53\t  return bytesToB64std(enc.encode(str)).replace(/\\+/g, \"-\").replace(/\\//g, \"_\").replace(/=+$/g, \"\");\n54\t}\n55\tfunction toHex(buf) {\n56\t  var v = new Uint8Array(buf), out = \"\";\n57\t  for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) { out += v[i].toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"); }\n58\t  return out;\n59\t}\n60\tasync function sha256Hex(bytes) {                     // == sigil.reuse.sha256_hex\n61\t  return toHex(await crypto.subtle.digest(\"SHA-256\", bytes));\n62\t}\n63\t\n64\t/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IndexedDB */\n65\t/* One DB, two stores: \"kv\" (device key material + the monotonic nonce) and \"outbox\" (POSTs queued\n66\t * while offline, flushed on reconnect). CryptoKey objects are structured-cloneable, so the\n67\t * NON-EXTRACTABLE private key persists here without ever exposing its bytes. */\n68\tvar DB = null;\n69\tfunction openDB() {\n70\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n71\t    var req = indexedDB.open(\"sigil-companion\", 1);\n72\t    req.onupgradeneeded = function () {\n73\t      var db = req.result;\n74\t      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(\"kv\")) { db.createObjectStore(\"kv\", { keyPath: \"k\" }); }\n75\t      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(\"outbox\")) { db.createObjectStore(\"outbox\", { keyPath: \"id\", autoIncrement: true }); }\n76\t    };\n77\t    req.onsuccess = function () { resolve(req.result); };\n78\t    req.onerror = function () { reject(req.error); };\n79\t  });\n80\t}\n81\tfunction tx(store, mode) { return DB.transaction(store, mode).objectStore(store); }\n82\tfunction idbGet(store, key) {\n83\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n84\t    var r = tx(store, \"readonly\").get(key);\n85\t    r.onsuccess = function () { resolve(r.result); };\n93\t    r.onerror = function () { reject(r.error); };\n94\t  });\n95\t}\n96\tfunction idbDel(store, key) {\n97\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n98\t    var r = tx(store, \"readwrite\").delete(key);\n99\t    r.onsuccess = function () { resolve(); }\n116\t  try {\n117\t    var k = await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name: \"Ed25519\" }, false, [\"sign\", \"verify\"]);\n118\t    return !!(k && k.privateKey && k.publicKey);\n119\t  } catch (e) { return false; We only\n136\t  // ever exportKey(\"raw\") the PUBLIC key.\n137\t  var pair = await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name: \"Ed25519\" }, false, [\"sign\", \"verify\"]);\n138\t  var raw = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey(\"raw\", pair.publicKey));  // 32 bytes\n139\t  var pubB64 = bytesToB64std(raw);\n140\t  var short = (await sha256Hex(enc.encode(pubB64))).slice(0, 16);\n141\t  var fingerprint = [short.slice(0, 4), short.slice(4, 8), short.slice(8, 12), short.slice(12, 16)].join(\"-\");\n142\t  var deviceId = randomDeviceId();\n143\t  DEVICE = { privateKey: pair.privateKey, pubB64: pubB64, deviceId: deviceId, fingerprint: fingerprint };\n144\t  await idbPut(\"kv\", { k: \"device\", privateKey: pair.privateKey, pubB64: pubB64, deviceId: deviceId, fingerprint: fingerprint });\n145\t  await idbPut(\"kv\", { k: \"nonce\", v: 0 });\n146\t  return DEVICE;\n147\t}\n148\t\n149\tasync function nextNonce() {                          // monotonic, per-device, persisted (replay gate)\n150\t  var rec = await idbGet(\"kv\", \"nonce\");\n151\t  var n = (rec && typeof rec.v === \"number\" ? (\"?\" + extraQuery) : \"\");\n190\t  var r = await fetch(url, { headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Envelope\": env }, cache: \"no-store\" });\n191\t  if (!r.ok) { on401(r.status); throw new Error(\"GET \" + path + \" -> \" + r.status); }\n192\t  clearBanner();\n193\t  return r.json();\n194\t}\n195\t\n196\tasync function effectfulPost(path, action, args) {    // panic / relay — signed, replay-gated\n197\t  var env = await buildEnvelope(action, args || {}, await nextNonce());\n198\t  var req = { path: path, headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Envelope\": env }, body: \"\" };\n199\t  return deliver(req);\n200\t}\n201\t\n202\tasync function postApproval(seq, decision) {          // device-signed governor.approval -> /api/action\n203\t  var approver = \"device\";\n204\t  var msgBytes = enc.encode(SigilCanonical.canonicalJson({ approver: approver, decision: decision, target: seq }));\n205\t  var sig = await signBytes(msgBytes);\n206\t  var payload = {\n207\t    signal: \"governor.approval\", approval: decision, target_seq: seq,\n208\t    approver: approver, reason: \"\", pubkey: DEVICE.pubB64, sig: sig,\n209\t    msg_digest: await sha256Hex(msgBytes), device: true\n210\t  };\n211\t  var req = { path: \"/api/action\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) };\n212\t  return deliver(req);\n213\t}\n214\t\n215\t/* deliver: try now; if the network is unreachable, QUEUE it (already signed) and flush on reconnect.\n216\t * If the server RESPONDS (any HTTP status) the item is considered delivered — the server adjudicated. */\n217\tasync function deliver(req) {\n218\t  try {\n219\t    var r = await fetch(req.path, {\n220\t      method: \"POST\", headers: req.headers, body: req.body, cache: \"no-store\"\n221\t    });\n222\t    var j = await r.json().catch(function () { return {}; {"parentUuid":"06a62706-d372-4eea-bf92-ae91e099917a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q8zZpWQGmp4FbnSZK9ZCFk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use strict\";\n2\t/* SIGIL Companion — canonical JSON. The JS<->Python PARITY CONTRACT.\n3\t *\n4\t * Byte-identical to Python (sigil/reuse/canonical.py :: canonical_json):\n5\t *     json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\",\":\"), ensure_ascii=False).encode(\"utf-8\")\n6\t * This is the #1 correctness item: the desktop bridge verifies the device signature over\n7\t * exactly these bytes (sigil/bridge/envelope.py :: envelope_message), so the phone MUST\n8\t * reproduce them or every signature is rejected.\n9\t *\n10\t * PINNED PARITY VECTOR (from tests/test_bridge_envelope.py ::\n11\t *   test_envelope_message_fixed_vector_parity):\n12\t *     core = {\"v\":1,\"device\":\"DEVKEYB64\",\"action\":\"read:snapshot\",\"args\":{},\"nonce\":1,\"ts\":1700000000}\n13\t *     canonicalJson(core)  ===  the UTF-8 bytes of the string\n14\t *     {\"action\":\"read:snapshot\",\"args\":{},\"device\":\"DEVKEYB64\",\"nonce\":1,\"ts\":1700000000,\"v\":1}\n15\t *\n16\t * Why plain JSON.stringify is safe for the LEAVES: over the SIGIL value domain — ASCII object\n17\t * keys; strings; INTEGER numbers only (app.js floors ts to whole seconds and uses integer\n18\t * nonces / seqs — floats are NEVER signed, because float repr can diverge between the two\n19\t * runtimes); booleans; null; arrays — ECMAScript JSON.stringify emits bytes identical to Python\n20\t * json.dumps(ensure_ascii=False): the same string escapes (\\\" \\\\ \\b \\t \\n \\f \\r, all other\n21\t * control chars as lowercase \\u00xx, non-ASCII left raw, \"/\" NOT escaped), the same integer\n22\t * rendering, and the same true/false/null tokens. The ONLY two things json.dumps does that plain\n23\t * JSON.stringify does not are (1) recursive KEY SORTING and (2) the compact \",\"/\":\" separators\n24\t * with no spaces — which is precisely, and only, what this function adds.\n25\t *\n26\t * Exported for BOTH the browser (window.SigilCanonical) and Node (module.exports) so the parity\n27\t * is falsifiable: tests/test_bridge_webapp.py runs this exact function under Node and asserts the\n28\t * bytes equal Python's envelope_message for the fixed vector.\n29\t */\n30\t(function (root, factory) {\n31\t  var api = factory();\n32\t  if (typeof module !== \"undefined\" && module.exports) { module.exports = api; }   // Node\n33\t  else { root.SigilCanonical = api; }                                              // browser / worker\n34\t})(typeof self !== \"undefined\" ? self : this, function () {\n35\t  function canonicalJson(value) {\n36\t    if (value === null || typeof value !== \"object\") {\n37\t      // string / number / boolean / null — JSON.stringify matches Python for the SIGIL domain\n38\t      return JSON.stringify(value);\n39\t    }\n40\t    if (Array.isArray(value)) {\n41\t      var parts = [];\n42\t      for (var i = 0; i < value.length; i++) { parts.push(canonicalJson(value[i])); }\n43\t      return \"[\" + parts.join(\",\") + \"]\";\n44\t    }\n45\t    // object: sort keys (code-unit order == Unicode code-point order for ASCII keys), compact\n46\t    var keys = Object.keys(value).sort();\n47\t    var out = \"\";\n48\t    for (var j = 0; j < keys.length; j++) {\n49\t      var k = keys[j];\n50\t      if (j) { out += \",\"; }\n51\t      out += JSON.stringify(k) + \":\" + canonicalJson(value[k]);\n52\t    }\n53\t    return \"{\" + out + \"}\";\n54\t  }\n55\t  return { canonicalJson: canonicalJson };\n56\t});\n57\t"}]},"uuid":"ce48ee12-988a-41b1-bb55-22f7b25a7ae4","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:29.593Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"06a62706-d372-4eea-bf92-ae91e099917a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"71893c39-1563-4b77-9d1f-21d7cccc90d7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018UFStvEw8dBeNc3xSVeMAC","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use strict\";\n2\t/* SIGIL Companion service worker — a cache-first OFFLINE SHELL for the static app only.\n3\t *\n4\t * Scope is /static/ (the only path the unmodified bridge server serves this file from — see\n5\t * sigil/bridge/server.py :: _serve_static). The installed app's start_url therefore also lives under\n6\t * /static/ (manifest.json) so the shell is genuinely reloadable offline.\n7\t *\n8\t * DOCTRINE: it caches ONLY the static shell (html/js/css/manifest). It NEVER caches an /api/*\n9\t * response — bridge reads/actions must always be LIVE and freshly signed; a stale cached snapshot or\n10\t * approval would be a lie over the tunnel. So /api/* and every POST fall straight through to the\n11\t * network. The app can still SIGN approvals offline (WebCrypto + IndexedDB in app.js, no network) and\n12\t * flush the queued, already-signed POSTs on reconnect — that offline capability lives in app.js, not\n13\t * here; the SW's only job is to make the app itself load without a connection.\n14\t */\n15\tvar CACHE = \"sigil-companion-v1\";\n16\tvar SHELL = [\n17\t  \"/static/index.html\",\n18\t  \"/static/app.js\",\n19\t  \"/static/canonical.js\",\n20\t  \"/static/style.css\",\n21\t  \"/static/manifest.json\"\n22\t];\n23\t\n24\tself.addEventListener(\"install\", function (e) {\n25\t  e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(function (c) { return c.addAll(SHELL); }).then(function () { return self.skipWaiting(); }));\n26\t});\n27\t\n28\tself.addEventListener(\"activate\", function (e) {\n29\t  e.waitUntil(\n30\t    caches.keys().then(function (keys) {\n31\t      return Promise.all(keys.map(function (k) { return k === CACHE ? null : caches.delete(k); }));\n32\t    }).then(function () { return self.clients.claim(); })\n33\t  );\n34\t});\n35\t\n36\tself.addEventListener(\"fetch\", function (e) {\n37\t  var req = e.request;\n38\t  if (req.method !== \"GET\") { return; }                 // never intercept POST actions\n39\t  var url = new URL(req.url);\n40\t  if (url.pathname.indexOf(\"/api/\") === 0) { return; }  // never cache live bridge data — go to network\n41\t  // cache-first for the shell, with a network refresh in the background (stale-while-revalidate)\n42\t  e.respondWith(\n43\t    caches.match(req).then(function (hit) {\n44\t      var net = fetch(req).then(function (resp) {\n45\t        if (resp && resp.ok && (req.url.indexOf(\"/static/\") !== -1 || url.pathname === \"/\")) {\n46\t          var copy = resp.clone();\n47\t          caches.open(CACHE).then(function (c) { c.put(req, copy); });\n48\t        }\n49\t        return resp;\n50\t      }).catch(function () { return hit; });\n51\t      return hit || net;\n52\t    })\n53\t  );\n54\t});\n55\t"}]},"uuid":"c3531299-a968-4bbf-b6c7-e5d6ea317e82","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:30.156Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"71893c39-1563-4b77-9d1f-21d7cccc90d7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
