{"parentUuid":"3104985c-001f-4186-8b6d-0118cfe8fb95","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VfmXRFqS5NeAnEhueKVi1N","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\",\n27\t             \"has_hid_inject\", \"has_camera_stream\")\n28\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n32\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n33\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n34\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n35\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"]),\n36\t            \"has_hid_inject\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_hid_inject\", False)),\n37\t            \"has_camera_stream\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_camera_stream\", False))}\n38\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n39\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n43\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n44\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n45\t    latest: dict = {}\n46\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n47\t        p = r.payload\n48\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n49\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n50\t    return latest\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n54\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n55\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n56\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n57\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n61\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n62\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n63\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n67\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n68\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n69\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n70\t    state: dict = {}\n71\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n72\t        p = r.payload\n73\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n74\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n75\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n76\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tclass DeviceApprover:\n80\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n81\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n82\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n83\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n84\t        self.store = store\n85\t        self.device_key = device_key\n86\t\n87\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n88\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n89\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n90\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n91\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n92\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n93\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"249bbeb7-58bc-4ce6-958b-d828620bfc9a","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:30.768Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3104985c-001f-4186-8b6d-0118cfe8fb95","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"334d98b3-0278-41df-a1e4-29b3fb530bab","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0188bcrC85SP9SnSBwEG1rNX","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only supersession + current-view read (ported from agents/blackboard.py's\n2\tpost/supersede/read discipline). The spine is never edited; a revised fact is SUPERSEDED by\n3\ta later record whose `supersedes_id` names it. `iter_current` returns the live view — the\n4\tconsolidation records that nothing later has superseded — which is what the MCP tools serve.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import Iterator\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..spine.models import SpineRecord\n10\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n11\tfrom .grounding import CONSOLIDATE_SOURCE\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef consolidation_records(store: SpineStore, kinds: set[str] | None = None) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n15\t    \"\"\"Every record the ARCHIVIST wrote (source=archivist), optionally filtered by kind.\"\"\"\n16\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n17\t        if r.source == CONSOLIDATE_SOURCE and (kinds is None or r.kind in kinds):\n18\t            yield r\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef iter_current(store: SpineStore, kinds: set[str] | None = None) -> list[SpineRecord]:\n22\t    \"\"\"The live view: consolidation records of `kinds` that nothing later has superseded.\"\"\"\n23\t    recs = list(consolidation_records(store, kinds))\n24\t    superseded = {r.supersedes_id for r in recs if r.supersedes_id is not None}\n25\t    return [r for r in recs if r.seq not in superseded]\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef promotion_ledgers(store: SpineStore) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:\n29\t    \"\"\"Two SEPARATE idempotency ledgers: keys already promoted as GROUNDED facts, and keys\n30\t    already recorded as demoted refusals. Kept apart so a prior DEMOTE never blocks a later\n31\t    GROUNDED promotion of the same fact — demote-only must not become demote-permanent.\"\"\"\n32\t    grounded: set[str] = set()\n33\t    refused: set[str] = set()\n34\t    for r in consolidation_records(store):\n35\t        k = r.payload.get(\"promotion_key\")\n36\t        if k is None:\n37\t            continue\n38\t        (refused if r.kind == \"refusal\" else grounded).add(k)\n39\t    return grounded, refused\n40\t"}]},"uuid":"b12b540f-08fb-4988-85bc-11612585e226","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:45.671Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"334d98b3-0278-41df-a1e4-29b3fb530bab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"b12b540f-08fb-4988-85bc-11612585e226","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAUfgTjLqj4pcShnMZdR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0161nHAETgAHCdjUJjLqZmqT","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/recall.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":15039,"cache_read_input_tokens":90540,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":15039,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUfbhjRHTAjcPZmWcR1","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"f5e1875c-0a9a-469d-aeb0-aaf9f827fd61","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:46.164Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f5e1875c-0a9a-469d-aeb0-aaf9f827fd61","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0161nHAETgAHCdjUJjLqZmqT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Perception recall (Phase 7, WS-A V-iv) — \"where did I last see X?\" answered from GROUNDED\n2\tperception history. Scans perception `event` records for the most recent whose authoritative OCR\n3\t`captured_text` contains the subject (all its salient tokens), and serves the VERBATIM OCR span +\n4\tframe ref + timestamp — the owner's own on-screen text, never a paraphrase and never an advisory VLM\n5\tlead. A0 (observe). Reuses the `consolidate.gate` grounding tokenizer + verbatim-span extractor so\n6\trecall grounds exactly like the memory gate.\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import Optional\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..consolidate.gate import salient\n12\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n13\t\n14\tPERCEPTION_SIGNAL = \"perception\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef _grounded_line(subject_tokens: set[str], captured: str) -> Optional[str]:\n18\t    \"\"\"The verbatim OCR LINE that contains ALL the subject's salient tokens (so the served sighting\n19\t    always actually shows the subject), or None if no single line holds them (red-pen BLOCK-4 — a\n20\t    whole-frame token match whose tokens are scattered across lines is NOT a grounded sighting).\"\"\"\n21\t    for line in captured.splitlines():\n22\t        if line.strip() and subject_tokens <= salient(line):\n23\t            return line.strip()\n24\t    return None\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef recall(store: SpineStore, subject: str) -> Optional[dict]:\n28\t    \"\"\"The most recent grounded sighting of `subject`, or None. Grounded = every salient token of the\n29\t    subject appears in that frame's captured OCR text (the authoritative ground truth).\"\"\"\n30\t    subj = salient(subject)\n31\t    if not subj:\n32\t        return None\n33\t    latest = None\n34\t    latest_line = None\n35\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n36\t        if r.kind != \"event\" or r.payload.get(\"signal\") != PERCEPTION_SIGNAL:\n37\t            continue\n38\t        line = _grounded_line(subj, r.payload.get(\"captured_text\") or \"\")\n39\t        if line is not None:                  # subject co-located on a real OCR line → a grounded sighting\n40\t            latest, latest_line = r, line     # seq-ascending iteration → last match is most recent\n41\t    if latest is None:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    return {\n44\t        \"seq\": latest.seq, \"entry_hash\": latest.entry_hash, \"when\": latest.ts,\n45\t        \"frame_sha256\": latest.payload.get(\"frame_sha256\"),\n46\t        \"quote\": latest_line,                 # verbatim OCR line that actually contains the subject\n47\t    }\n48\t"}]},"uuid":"8755f8bb-9411-4d8f-8681-2425dfcb1b4f","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:46.197Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f5e1875c-0a9a-469d-aeb0-aaf9f827fd61","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Signing helpers are\n5\tprovisioning-only; the runtime only ever verifies. The sole change from the source is a\n6\tlocal `IntegrityError` in place of the framework's `EntitlementError` (decoupling).\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport base64\n11\timport binascii\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\t\n14\tfrom cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature\n15\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (\n16\t    Ed25519PrivateKey,\n17\t    Ed25519PublicKey,\n18\t)\n19\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.serialization import (\n20\t    Encoding,\n21\t    NoEncryption,\n22\t    PrivateFormat,\n23\t    PublicFormat,\n24\t)\n25\t\n26\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, Signature, TrustRoot\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass IntegrityError(Exception):\n30\t    \"\"\"Malformed key/signature material (a provisioning error, not an attacker path).\"\"\"\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _b64decode_exact(value: str, expected_len: int, what: str) -> bytes:\n34\t    try:\n35\t        raw = base64.b64decode(value, validate=True)\n36\t    except (ValueError, binascii.Error) as e:\n37\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} is not valid base64: {e}\") from e\n38\t    if len(raw) != expected_len:\n39\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} decodes to {len(raw)} bytes, expected {expected_len}\")\n40\t    return raw\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef load_public_key(public_key_b64: str) -> Ed25519PublicKey:\n44\t    return Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(_b64decode_exact(public_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 public key\"))\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef verify_one(public_key_b64: str, message: bytes, signature_b64: str) -> bool:\n48\t    \"\"\"True iff a valid Ed25519 signature; False on bad sig; raises only on malformed material.\"\"\"\n49\t    pub = load_public_key(public_key_b64)\n50\t    sig = _b64decode_exact(signature_b64, 64, \"Ed25519 signature\")\n51\t    try:\n52\t        pub.verify(sig, message)\n53\t        return True\n54\t    except InvalidSignature:\n55\t        return False\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n59\tclass ThresholdResult:\n60\t    satisfied: bool\n61\t    valid_signers: tuple[str, ...]\n62\t    threshold: int\n63\t    reason: str\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef verify_threshold(message: bytes, signatures: list[Signature], trust_root: TrustRoot) -> ThresholdResult:\n67\t    \"\"\"Count distinct trust-root authorisers with a valid signature; compare to threshold.\"\"\"\n68\t    by_id: dict[str, AuthorizerKey] = {a.key_id: a for a in trust_root.authorizers}\n69\t    valid: list[str] = []\n70\t    seen: set[str] = set()\n71\t    for sig in signatures:\n72\t        if sig.key_id in seen:\n73\t            continue\n74\t        seen.add(sig.key_id)\n75\t        authorizer = by_id.get(sig.key_id)\n76\t        if authorizer is None:\n77\t            continue\n78\t        if verify_one(authorizer.public_key_b64, message, sig.signature_b64):\n79\t            valid.append(sig.key_id)\n80\t    satisfied = len(valid) >= trust_root.threshold\n81\t    reason = (\n82\t        f\"{len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) >= threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n83\t        if satisfied else\n84\t        f\"only {len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) < threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n85\t    )\n86\t    return ThresholdResult(satisfied=satisfied, valid_signers=tuple(valid), threshold=trust_root.threshold, reason=reason)\n87\t\n88\t\n89\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n90\tclass KeyPair:\n91\t    public_key_b64: str\n92\t    private_key_b64: str\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tdef generate_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n96\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()\n97\t    raw_priv = priv.private_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PrivateFormat.Raw, NoEncryption())\n98\t    raw_pub = priv.public_key().public_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PublicFormat.Raw)\n99\t    return KeyPair(\n100\t        public_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_pub).decode(\"ascii\"),\n101\t        private_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_priv).decode(\"ascii\"),\n102\t    )\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tdef sign(private_key_b64: str, message: bytes) -> str:\n106\t    raw = _b64decode_exact(private_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 private key\")\n107\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(raw)\n108\t    return base64.b64encode(priv.sign(message)).decode(\"ascii\")\n109\t"}]},"uuid":"9210c9d4-fc36-4ece-bb4f-1e0492b23d72","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:35:13.807Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1e97eea5-4b30-429e-b9aa-cf7e23f5a2bb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"a67724fe-8217-4e93-900c-4edf608bb8b1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MFoqfwFB8vUpfyUJYatzGq","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"SIGIL Phase 9 W2-K — device-signed REMOTE ARM of a gesture session (the deliberate, reviewed\ntrust-widening). Proves the full threat table: only an owner-authorized device can arm; forged /\nreplayed / stale / after-revoke arms are refused; the TTL is clamped shorter than a local arm; a device\narm never displaces a live session; the owner's kill-switch/disarm always wins; and every downstream\nbound (A1-inject / A2-queue) is UNCHANGED for a device-armed session.\nRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_gesture_arm.py\"\"\"\nimport tempfile\nimport time\n\nfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\nfrom sigil.bridge.daemon import BridgeDaemon\nfrom sigil.gesture.components import RecordingInputBackend\nfrom sigil.gesture.session import MAX_DEVICE_TTL, SESSION_ARMED, SessionGate, arm_request_message, sign_arm_request\nfrom sigil.gesture.types import GestureIntent\nfrom sigil.mesh import authorize_device, revoke_device\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair, verify_one\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nOWNER = generate_keypair()\nOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\nDEV = generate_keypair()          # a phone the owner will authorize\nATTACKER = generate_keypair()     # a device the owner NEVER authorized\n\n\ndef _store():\n    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n\n\nclass FakeCls:\n    \"\"\"hid.pointer.* → A1 (inject in-session); hid.type/combo/launch → A2 (queue). No kernel subprocess.\"\"\"\n    def classify(self, tool):\n        return Tier.A1 if tool.startswith(\"hid.pointer\") else Tier.A2\n\n\ndef _gate(store, backend=None):\n    return SessionGate(store, backend or RecordingInputBackend(), classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n\n\ndef _authorize(store, kp=DEV, device_id=\"phone1\"):\n    authorize_device(store, device_id, kp.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n\n\ndef _req(kp=DEV, *, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=1, ts=None, ttl=120.0):\n    return sign_arm_request(kp, device_id=device_id, nonce=nonce, ts=ts if ts is not None else time.time(), ttl_seconds=ttl)\n\n\n# ---- happy path + TTL clamp ----------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef test_authorized_device_arms_and_ttl_is_clamped():\n    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n    now = time.time()\n    sess = g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now, ttl=120.0), now=now)\n    assert sess is not None and sess.live, \"an authorized, fresh, signed request arms a session\"\n    assert abs(sess.expires_at - (now + 120.0)) < 1e-6, \"ttl within MAX is honored\"\n    # a request over the cap clamps to MAX_DEVICE_TTL (a trust-narrowing inside the widening)\n    s2 = _store(); _authorize(s2); g2 = _gate(s2)\n    sess2 = g2.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now, ttl=99999.0), now=now)\n    assert abs(sess2.expires_at - (now + MAX_DEVICE_TTL)) < 1e-6, f\"ttl clamped to {MAX_DEVICE_TTL}\"\n\n\n# ---- the threat table (each refusal records a refusal + returns None + arms nothing) --------------\n=== blackboard read/current supersede logic ===","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"1ae9e83b-f843-4ce7-9983-610cd6c6c964","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:35:17.870Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a67724fe-8217-4e93-900c-4edf608bb8b1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d7efbb78-2392-4247-a2af-6973a71ac9fb","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAUppuE6eSr5Pw4xCRLP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01KPWaBHbWmEWbrb1wgTaSuB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/r3.py","content":"import sys, tempfile, time, os\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/kali/sigil\")\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\n\nOWNER = generate_keypair()\n\ndef build_spine(n):\n    p = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\n    s = SpineStore(p)\n    for i in range(n):\n        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n                 payload={\"signal\": \"gesture.action\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": \"A1\",\n                          \"tool\": \"hid.pointer.move\", \"session_id\": \"x\", \"summary\": \"move\"})\n    return p\n\nfor n in (1000, 10000, 40000):\n    p = build_spine(n)\n    size_mb = os.path.getsize(p) / 1e6\n    s = SpineStore(p)\n    ks = KillSwitch(s, owner_key=OWNER, trusted_pubkey=OWNER.public_key_b64)\n    # warm + time a single is_engaged() (the per-intent Phase-9 consult)\n    ks.is_engaged()\n    t0 = time.perf_counter()\n    reps = 5\n    for _ in range(reps):\n        ks.is_engaged()\n    dt = (time.perf_counter() - t0) / reps\n    max_fps = 1.0 / dt if dt > 0 else float(\"inf\")\n    print(f\"n={n:6d} spine={size_mb:6.1f}MB  is_engaged()={dt*1000:8.2f} ms/call  \"\n          f\"-> max {max_fps:8.1f} scans/sec (target: 30 fps => {30*dt*1000:7.1f} ms/sec just on the kill-switch)\")\n    os.remove(p)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":380,"cache_read_input_tokens":120576,"output_tokens":1305,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":380},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1305,"cache_read_input_tokens":120576,"cache_creation_input_tokens":380,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":380,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUpoC3Km8b5BackUns1","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"eb81fdb4-2ac8-4c71-8bac-52cab21e1db1","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:37:02.703Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"82e093cb-c04c-4c45-97fc-20323b735291","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAUxAcb11nynaakrLfH2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Kmvdc8xMh9W8Cnrr8bFrV4","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/r5.py","content":"import sys, tempfile, time, threading\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/home/kali/sigil\")\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\nfrom sigil.bridge.envelope import build_core, sign_envelope, consume, device_nonce_highwater\n\nDEV = generate_keypair(); AUTH = {DEV.public_key_b64}\npath = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\nSpineStore(path).append(kind=\"event\", source=\"x\", actor=\"x\", payload={\"seed\": 1})  # genesis\n\nprint(\"=== A. concurrent-append chain FORK (fresh SpineStore per request thread, no lock) ===\")\n# Mirror the server: every handler thread builds its OWN SpineStore(self.spine_path).\ndef worker(tag, barrier, results):\n    s = SpineStore(path)                 # fresh store, like self.server.store()\n    barrier.wait()                       # line them up so both read the same tip\n    seq = s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload={\"who\": tag})\n    results.append((tag, seq))\n\nbarrier = threading.Barrier(8)\nresults = []\nts = [threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(i, barrier, results)) for i in range(8)]\nfor t in ts: t.start()\nfor t in ts: t.join()\nseqs = [seq for _, seq in results]\nprint(\"returned seqs:\", sorted(seqs), \"-> DISTINCT?\", len(set(seqs))==len(seqs))\nok, msg = SpineStore(path).verify()\nprint(\"chain verify after 8 concurrent appends:\", ok, \"|\", msg)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== B. nonce replay-gate TOCTOU (two threads read highwater before either writes a receipt) ===\")\npath2 = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\nSpineStore(path2).append(kind=\"event\", source=\"x\", actor=\"x\", payload={\"seed\": 1})\nenv = sign_envelope(DEV, build_core(DEV.public_key_b64, \"relay\", {\"text\":\"drain\"}, nonce=9, ts=time.time()))\naccepted = []\ndef eff(barrier):\n    s = SpineStore(path2)\n    try:\n        # decompose consume() to expose the check-then-act window both threads hit in practice\n        hw = device_nonce_highwater(s, DEV.public_key_b64)   # READ\n        barrier.wait()                                       # both threads have now read hw=0\n        if 9 <= hw:                                          # replay gate\n            return\n        from sigil.bridge.envelope import record_receipt, verify_envelope\n        ok, core = verify_envelope(env, AUTH)\n        record_receipt(s, core)                              # WRITE\n        accepted.append(9)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(\"err\", e)\nb2 = threading.Barrier(2)\nt1 = threading.Thread(target=eff, args=(b2,)); t2 = threading.Thread(target=eff, args=(b2,))\nt1.start(); t2.start(); t1.join(); t2.join()\nprint(f\"same effectful nonce=9 accepted {len(accepted)} times (replay gate defeated if >1)\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":573,"cache_read_input_tokens":126247,"output_tokens":3331,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":573},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3331,"cache_read_input_tokens":126247,"cache_creation_input_tokens":573,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":573,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUx9A2jDKppKxTw1Ze6","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"20783169-d974-4b10-908d-48563903bb69","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:39:13.138Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3083d187-f366-4b2a-9fc4-dfe6b1080957","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ab85e5582d27cf290","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAqsKeNFnFGnpfKYNRyN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# Independent Adversarial Review — SIGIL Phase 9 \"COMPANION\" (branch `phase9-companion`)\n\nSecond pass, disjoint from the primary red-pen. I read the full diff and every named file, and built runnable repros under the scratchpad (run with `~/.sigil/venv/bin/python`) to prove each defect. **Full suite after review: 297 pass / 0 fail / 0 error.**\n\nNine findings survived, ranked by severity. Three are proven HIGH. The strongest transport/crypto attacks the primary reviewer owns (forge/rebind/replay-on-the-envelope) genuinely held — I say precisely where at the end.\n\n---\n\n## HIGH-1 — Per-request/per-intent O(n) full-spine scan is an unbounded self-DoS that also degrades the panic guarantee\n\n`SessionGate.handle` now consults the kill-switch on **every** intent (`sigil/gesture/session.py:196-197`), and `KillSwitch.is_engaged` (`sigil/governor/killswitch.py:38-49`) does a **full `iter_records()` scan** of the append-only spine. Measured on a synthetic spine (the real one is ~43k records per your memory notes):\n\n```\nn=  1000 spine= 0.6MB  is_engaged()=  10.18 ms/call -> max  98 scans/sec\nn= 10000 spine= 5.6MB  is_engaged()= 101.19 ms/call -> max  10 scans/sec\nn= 40000 spine=22.4MB  is_engaged()= 407.96 ms/call -> max   2.5 scans/sec\n```\n\n`handle` is invoked per intent, and pointer `move` intents fire at up to 30fps — so at 40k records the kill-switch consult **alone** demands ~12.2 s of CPU per 1 s of gesture input (12x saturation). This is **not incremental** over the pre-existing per-intent cost: `KernelClassifier.classify()` is a subprocess but measures **1.00 ms/call and is constant in spine size**; the new scan is 10–400x larger and grows without bound.\n\nTwo consequences: (a) gesture control becomes unusable on any mature spine, and (b) the docstring's core safety promise — \"an owner halt instantly neuters injection mid-session… critical for a device-armed session the owner may not be watching\" — is false: panic latency is now proportional to spine size and starved by the scan backlog. The same pattern is **server-wide**, not just gestures: every GET calls `_authed → _authorized_now → authorized_devices()` (full scan, `server.py:121-123`); effectful POSTs add `consume`'s highwater scan; `recall/snapshot/pending/record` each add more. A single `/api/snapshot` is several full-spine passes.\n\n- **Doctrine violated:** kill-switch \"read fresh per decision so a release takes effect immediately\" (killswitch.py:8) — but \"immediately\" is now O(spine).\n- **Fix:** cache `is_engaged` per `handle` batch with a short TTL keyed off the spine tip seq (append is O(1) — track a tip cursor and only rescan tail-since-last-check), or maintain a cheap kill-state watermark. Reads of the killswitch/authorized-device set should scan tail-from-last-known, not genesis. Quantify the latency you're willing to give the panic path and cap the cache TTL below it (e.g. 100 ms).\n\n## HIGH-2 — Concurrent writes corrupt the hash-chained spine (Phase 9 exposes it by putting write endpoints behind a threaded server)\n\n`BridgeServer` is a `ThreadingHTTPServer` with `daemon_threads = True` (`server.py:72-73`) and builds a **fresh `SpineStore` per request** (`server.py:97-98`). `SpineStore.append` (`store.py:49-67`) is an unlocked read-tip → compute-digest → write sequence. Eight concurrent handler threads each read tip=seq0 and all write seq=1 — **stable across 3 runs**:\n\n```\nreturned seqs: [1,1,1,1,1,1,1,1] -> DISTINCT? False\nchain verify after 8 concurrent appends: False | chain break at seq 1: prev_hash mismatch\n```\n\nAny two near-simultaneous **writes** (panic+relay, two approvals, approval+arm) fork the tamper-evident chain and permanently break `verify()`/`checkpoint`. `SpineStore` predates Phase 9, but Phase 9 is the first component to expose it — it adds four network-reachable, concurrently-servable write endpoints (`/api/panic`, `/api/relay`, `/api/action`, `/api/gesture/arm`). Reads don't corrupt (they don't append), so the trigger is write-write overlap — possible but not every request; I rate impact HIGH, likelihood MEDIUM, and I'm being honest that my repro forces the worst-case window with a barrier.\n\n- **Doctrine violated:** \"append-only, hash-chained spine… tamper-evident\" — broken by ordinary concurrent use, no attacker needed.\n- **Fix:** serialize appends (process-wide `threading.Lock` around read-tip→write, or `fcntl.flock` on the file with a re-read of the tip inside the lock). A single shared, lock-guarded `SpineStore` for the server rather than fresh-per-request would also fix HIGH-1's redundant construction.\n\n## HIGH-3 (honesty) — The device-signed remote-arm path is INERT end-to-end\n\nWave 4's headline feature (\"device-signed remote arm — trust-widening\") is built but never invoked. `submit_arm_request` (`daemon.py:80`) records a `gesture.arm_request` on the spine; `arm_by_device` (`session.py:119`) is the enforcer. **Nothing in production connects them:**\n\n- `grep` for `arm_by_device` callers → **only `tests/test_gesture_arm.py`**. No runtime code reads a recorded `gesture.arm_request` and calls `arm_by_device`.\n- The PWA (`app.js`) has **no arm control** — `data-act` values are approve/deny/panic/relay/recall/copy-* only; it never POSTs `/api/gesture/arm`.\n- `run_gesture` has no device-arm wiring: the remote-landmark path requires `auto_arm=False` + an **already-owner-armed local gate** (`run.py:13-22,52-55`; `remote.py` docstring is explicit that device-arm \"is deliberately NOT built here\").\n\nSo a phone can POST a valid signed arm request, get `{ok:true, seq}`, and **nothing ever arms**. The endpoint + enforcer + TTL-clamp + replay logic are real and tested in isolation, but the feature \"a phone can genuinely arm a gesture session\" does not work. State this plainly in the wiring status. (Corollary on the prompt's stale-arm question: since `arm_by_device` is never reached, a recorded request just sits there; *if* it were consumed, step 3 refuses `abs(now-ts) > 30s` — fail-safe — but the **record** endpoint has no ts check, so stale bodies record fine.)\n\n## MEDIUM-4 — Nonce replay-gate TOCTOU: same effectful nonce accepted twice under concurrency\n\nSame root cause as HIGH-2, but the security-relevant consequence. `consume` (`envelope.py:97-107`) reads `device_nonce_highwater` then writes the receipt, unserialized. Two threads that both read highwater before either writes both pass `9 <= hw` — **stable across 3 runs**:\n\n```\nsame effectful nonce=9 accepted 2 times (replay gate defeated if >1)\n```\n\nAn on-tunnel attacker who captures one valid `panic`/`relay` envelope and fires it N times concurrently within the ±120 s freshness window gets it executed multiple times (double-relay a KERNEL command through WARDEN). Sequential replay is correctly blocked; only the concurrent window is open.\n\n- **Fix:** the same append serialization as HIGH-2, with the highwater check **inside** the lock (check-and-append atomic).\n\n## MEDIUM-5 — `/api/action` and `/api/gesture/arm` are unbounded spine-bloat sinks (no nonce, no dedup, no record-time freshness)\n\nUnlike panic/relay, the two body-credential endpoints (`server.py:365,383`) run no nonce gate. One captured signed body replays into unlimited records — proven:\n\n```\nreplayed ONE captured arm body 200x     -> +200 gesture.arm_request records\nreplayed ONE captured approval body 200x -> +200 governor.approval records (idempotent in pending() but unbounded growth)\n```\n\nReachable by anyone on the tunnel who captured one signed body, or by an authorized-but-misbehaving device. Approvals stay idempotent in `pending()` (target_seq keyed), so the impact is availability, not false approval — but it directly **amplifies HIGH-1** (every bloat record slows every future scan).\n\n- **Fix:** dedup at record time (reject a `gesture.arm_request`/`governor.approval` whose (pubkey, sig) already exists), or add a nonce/freshness gate to these endpoints too.\n\n## LOW/MEDIUM-6 — Unsigned extra fields are recorded verbatim into `gesture.arm_request` (`payload={**request}`)\n\n`_ARM_CORE` signs only `(signal, device_id, nonce, ts, ttl_seconds)` (`session.py:36`), but `submit_arm_request` records the **whole request** (`daemon.py:100-101`). Proven — a valid request with attacker-chosen unsigned keys is accepted and stored:\n\n```\nrecorded (UNSIGNED) device_pubkey: <VICTIM key>   ==VICTIM? True\nrecorded (UNSIGNED) session_id: attacker-chosen-sid\nrecorded (UNSIGNED) armed_by: device\n```\n\nNote the asymmetry vs the envelope: `verify_envelope` rebuilds the core as \"all fields except `sig`\" (`envelope.py:65`), so envelope extras break the signature and are rejected. The arm path uses a **fixed tuple**, so extras pass verification and are persisted. No live impact today (nothing consumes these records — see HIGH-3), but the recorded shape already carries `armed_by=\"device\"` + `device_pubkey` — one code-change away from `arm_by_device`'s replay-check fields (`session.py:154-155`). A latent footgun.\n\n- **Fix:** record only the signed core + `pubkey` + `sig` (mirror `record_receipt`, which correctly stores only routing fields), not `**request`.\n\n## LOW-7 — Nonce=0 lockout / off-by-one in the highwater default\n\n`device_nonce_highwater` returns 0 when no receipt exists (`envelope.py:82-94`) and `consume` rejects `nonce <= highwater` (`envelope.py:104`), so a client whose **first** effectful nonce is 0 is refused — proven:\n\n```\nnonce=0 REFUSED -> replay: nonce not fresh   (first effectful call locked out)\nnonce=1 accepted OK\n```\n\n`app.js` starts `nextNonce()` at 1 (`app.js:145,149-153`), so the shipped client is safe; but the docstring \"0 if none\" hides the fact that nonce 0 is permanently unusable — a trap for the parity contract / any non-JS client. Also confirms the **receipt-before-effect ordering**: the receipt is durable before the server runs `relay()`, so a relay that throws after `consume` returns **burns the nonce** (command lost, not replayable — fail-safe but a surprise; the client must bump-and-retry).\n\n- **Fix:** either sentinel the \"no receipt\" state as -1, or document that valid nonces start at 1.\n\n## LOW-8 — Offline outbox silently discards a refused panic/relay on reconnect\n\n`flushOutbox` (`app.js:230-243`) re-sends queued already-signed POSTs and `idbDel`s each one as soon as the fetch **resolves with any status** — including a 409 (stale/replay) or 401. A panic the owner tapped offline, flushed >120 s later, is refused as stale, deleted, and **not re-surfaced** (flush only calls `renderOutbox`). The button text warns about staleness at tap time (`app.js:400`), but the background flush swallows the actual refusal. For an emergency-stop this is a safety-relevant honesty gap.\n\n- **Fix:** on flush, inspect the response; if a queued panic/relay comes back non-ok, keep an explicit \"delivery FAILED — re-tap\" banner rather than silently dropping.\n\n## LOW-9 (functional, not security) — `bind_ok` rejects Tailscale's CGNAT range\n\n`bind_ok` is **sound for exposure** — I tested a full battery and every dangerous case is refused: `::ffff:8.8.8.8` (IPv4-mapped public) → False, `0.0.0.0`/`::`/`::ffff:0.0.0.0` (unspecified) → False, `224.0.0.1` (multicast) → False, `64:ff9b::8.8.8.8` (NAT64-embedded public) → False, all public v4/v6 → False. But `100.64.1.1` (CGNAT `100.64.0.0/10`) → **False**, and that is exactly the range Tailscale (a WireGuard mesh, which the docs repeatedly invoke) assigns. A Tailscale-bound bridge is refused. Link-local (`169.254/16`, `fe80::`) is allowed — harmless (non-routable). Zoned link-local (`fe80::1%wg0`) would also be refused (`ip_address` rejects the `%`).\n\n- **Fix:** if Tailscale is a supported transport, allow `100.64.0.0/10` explicitly; otherwise document the exclusion.\n\n---\n\n## What I attacked that HELD (so the primary reviewer isn't duplicated here)\n\n- **Envelope extra-key smuggling** — held. `verify_envelope` reconstructs core as \"everything but `sig`\", so any unsigned extra changes the canonical bytes and fails verification (contrast the arm path, LOW-6).\n- **XSS under CSP** — held. `app.js` routes all server data through `esc()` (&<> escaped) into text content; the only attribute sink (`data-seq`) takes integer seqs. `esc()` doesn't escape quotes, but that's saved by int-only attributes **and** the strict `default-src 'self'` CSP (no `unsafe-inline` → inline event handlers can't run, external loads blocked). Latent robustness gap, not exploitable as shipped.\n- **Service worker caching `/api/*`** — held. `service-worker.js:38-40` bails on non-GET and on any `/api/` path; only `/static/` + `/` shell is cached.\n- **Approval canonical parity** — held. `app.js:postApproval` signs `{approver,decision,target}`; server `_approval_message` builds `{target,decision,approver}`; key-sorted canonical JSON makes them byte-identical. `pubkey`/`sig` are standard-base64-with-padding, matching `verify_one`'s `validate=True` decode. `msg_digest` is recorded but not verified (unused, harmless).\n- **`supersedes_id` abuse** — held. `submit_device_approval` sets `supersedes_id=target_seq` (device-controlled), but the only supersession-honoring consumer, `consolidate/revise.iter_current` (`revise.py:21-25`), is **scoped to archivist-source records**; the device approval is `source=\"mesh\"`, so it can't supersede anything live. `pending()` resolves off the signed `target_seq`, not `supersedes_id`.\n- **bind_ok exposure bypass** — held (see LOW-9); no mapped/unspecified/public address slips through on Python 3.13.\n\nNet: the transport auth core is solid; the real risk in Phase 9 is (1) O(n)-scan self-DoS that also weakens the panic guarantee, (2) unserialized concurrent writes corrupting the chain / defeating the nonce gate behind the threaded server, and (3) the honesty gap that remote-arm doesn't actually work end-to-end."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":139825,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":5531,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":139825},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":5531,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":139825,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":139825,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":{"cache_miss_reason":{"type":"previous_message_not_found"}}},"requestId":"req_011CdAqsGvQfTdLrp765pRvs","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"845ddd06-839f-4319-8e62-95dcb32c8a18","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:14:01.944Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
